# ROLE: REVIEWER (codex)

You review the uncommitted changes in this repository against the task and acceptance criteria.
Inspect the diff with `git diff` and `git status`. Do NOT edit code.

Cover: correctness, security (HIPAA / multi-tenancy / auth), style-rule compliance, test coverage
(unit + e2e + stress), and missed edge cases. List each required change as a numbered actionable item.

Request changes only for concrete blockers: acceptance-criteria misses, regressions, money/security/PHI
risks, broken contracts, or missing tests for touched behavior. Do not block the run on speculative polish,
style preferences outside the project rules, or unrelated repo baseline failures; call those out as
follow-ups instead.

## Project context
ATRIA-X: multi-tenant SaaS for home-care agencies. React19+Vite8+TS, Tailwind4, React Router 7, Clerk (auth/orgs), Convex (backend+DB), Vitest, Playwright. Formatting: 2-space indent, single quotes, NO semicolons, PascalCase components, camelCase utils. CORE RULE: a service is never billed unless its shift documentation is complete and approved (caregiver->coordinator->billing). Multi-tenancy is the central invariant: every Convex fn touching tenant data MUST go through authHelpers.ts guards (requireTenant / requireTenantRole / assertTenantDoc); enforce auth twice (RouteGuard + server). Roles: org:admin, org:coordinator, org:caregiver. After editing convex/* run `npx convex codegen`. Never edit convex/_generated by hand. Follow CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md.

## Task
Commit all of Phase 1 with its 8 sessions into the main branch, and verify that the whole Phase 1 implementation matches perfectly what is written and specified in C:/Users/pinol/Documents/Work/atriax/phase-1-implementation-plan.md. Treat the existing dirty Phase 1 worktree as intentional cumulative session work. Do not reset, stash, or revert it. First verify the implementation against the plan and run the required gates. If and only if the implementation is green and matches the plan, commit all Phase 1 changes and merge/land them into main as requested. Report exact mismatches if any.

# ATRIA-X â€” Phase 1 Implementation Plan (Sessions + Prompts)

Author: Hermes (agent) Â· For: Diego Molina Silerio
Scope: build ALL of Phase 1 end-to-end â€” DB, backend, frontend (pixel-faithful to the
dark Figma), and the ADP time integration **fully built and plug-and-play** for when
credentials arrive.
Location: kept OUTSIDE `solid-pancake/` because the repo's pipeline-enforcement hook blocks
in-repo writes. Move into `docs/` later via the pipeline if you want it versioned.

Dev flow used throughout for this run: **plan/review (codex) â†’ implement (kimi) â†’ lint/typecheck/unit
gate â†’ codex review â†’ kimi fix loop â†’ e2e/stress gate**. When Claude/Opus is available again, switch
back to the stronger **opus+codex planning/review** flow.

### How to use this document â€” Hermes-ready mode
You do **not** need to run the Python pipeline command yourself.

For each implementation session below:
1. Open a **fresh Hermes session**.
2. Copy the block labeled **COPY/PASTE INTO A NEW HERMES SESSION**.
3. Paste it as the first message.
4. Hermes should load the ATRIA-X skills and run the multi-model pipeline internally using
   `--plan-mode codex`.
5. Wait for the run to finish. Hermes must report the pipeline run directory and read
   `solid-pancake/.hermes-pipeline/<ts>/summary.md` before claiming success.
6. Only move to the next session if lint, typecheck, tests/codegen gates are green or the failure
   is explicitly reported and accepted.

The prompts intentionally tell Hermes **not to edit the repo directly**. Hermes should run the
ATRIA-X pipeline for you; you only copy/paste the prompt.

---

## 0. What "Phase 1" is (locked scope)

Source of truth: `atriax/delivery-phases.md` (re-scope note 2026-06-21) + `atriax/adp-shift-flow-for-maria.md`.

Phase 1 = the **core daily loop**:

1. **Caregiver shift documentation with clock-in / clock-out.**
   - Clock-In **unlocks** the shift note (note cannot start before clock-in).
   - Clock-Out is **blocked** until the shift note is complete (no note, no clock-out).
   - Clock-in and clock-out are also optionally **geofence-locked**: when the agency enables
     the setting, the caregiver can only clock in/out at the patient's address or a configured
     shift-specific service address. Admins and coordinators can enable/disable this rule and
     configure the allowed address/radius.
   - Clock-in and clock-out times are sent to **ADP** as the caregiver's time punches for
     payroll. The shift note and raw location evidence stay in ATRIA-X and are **not** sent to ADP.
   - Order is always: **At the service location â†’ Clock In â†’ Shift Note â†’ Clock Out**.
2. **Coordinator review & approval.** Submitted notes â†’ review queue â†’ approve (marks the
   shift documented + billing-ready) or request correction (returns to caregiver to fix/resubmit).
3. **Caregiver identity & ADP time sync (only what the shift flow needs).**
   - **Initial load** of caregivers that already exist in ADP, so people match across systems.
   - **New caregiver created in ATRIA-X is reflected in ADP**, so they can clock in/out and be paid.
   - Just enough caregiver create/onboard for a caregiver to log in and run a shift. Full
     candidate application + HR onboarding is **Phase 2** â€” do not build it here.

Built **on the existing MVP** (Clerk auth, agency tenant, caregiver + coordinator roles, base
layout, shift lifecycle). We extend; we do not rebuild.

NOT in Phase 1 (defer): scheduling/availability, candidate portal, HR onboarding, compliance
engine, billing/invoicing + the full payroll/ADP export for a pay period (Phase 3),
notifications, reporting.


### Locked implementation decisions for this run
- Planning model: **codex only** for planning/review because Claude/Opus is unavailable until next month.
  Implementation/fix model remains **kimi**. When Opus is available again, switch `--plan-mode` back to
  `both` for the stronger opus+codex planning/review flow.
- Theme: **dark theme exactly matching Figma**.
- Geofence default: **off by default** for existing/demo agencies; admins/coordinators can enable it.
- Geofence management: **admins and coordinators** can enable/disable and configure rules/radius/address.
- Geofence enforcement points: **Clock-In and Clock-Out**.
- Default allowed radius: **150 meters**.
- Max accepted GPS accuracy: **100 meters**.
- Location target priority: **shift-specific service address overrides patient/client address**.
- Missing coordinates while geofence is enabled: **block clock-in/out** and show â€œaddress needs to be completed.â€
- ADP status: **no credentials yet**; build plug-and-play with `pending_credentials` queue mode.

---

## 1. The two decisions to make BEFORE coding (blockers)

These gate the build. Resolve both before Session 5 (FE) at the latest; ideally now.

### 1.1 Theme: dark (match Figma) vs keep light teal
The Figma set is **dark** (`atriax/design/design-system-tokens.md`). The live app is **light
teal** (`src/index.css`). "FE exactly as the Figma" â‡’ we re-skin the app to the **dark** token
set. The plan below assumes **dark to match Figma**. If you'd rather keep light, the FE
sessions still apply but you skip the dark-token port in Session 5 and the screens won't match
the Figma pixel-for-pixel. **Recommended: go dark to match Figma**, and add light later as a
second token mode (the token doc is already authored mode-swappable).

### 1.2 ADP product + time module (the one thing only ADP can confirm)
The ADP **auth model and worker APIs are stable and we build them concretely now.** The exact
**time-punch endpoint shape depends on which ADP product/time module Maria's agency uses**
(ADP Workforce Now Time & Attendance vs. a different Time module). We therefore build the
punch path behind an **adapter** (Section 3) so the unknown is isolated to one file you fill in
on the day credentials + product confirmation arrive â€” everything else is done and tested
against a mock. This is the "Do Not Build Yet: ADP payload/export spec" item from the docs,
handled correctly (isolated, not blocking).

Ask Maria/ADP to confirm, when they provision the dev account: (a) product = ADP Workforce
Now? (b) which Time module is in use, (c) whether punches are accepted via real-time event
API or batch import, (d) the sandbox base URL.

### 1.3 Geofence default behavior
Geofence should be **optional and agency-configurable**. The implementation below assumes:
- Default for existing/demo tenants: geofence enforcement **off** until an admin/coordinator enables it.
- When enabled, both Clock-In and Clock-Out require browser geolocation permission and server-side
  distance validation against the client service address or a shift-specific override.
- Default radius: **150 meters**; default max accepted browser accuracy: **100 meters**. Both values
  are tenant settings and can be adjusted by admins/coordinators.
- If the browser denies location or the device reports poor accuracy, the UI shows a plain-language
  blocked state and the Convex mutation rejects the punch.
- Store only operational evidence needed for audit: lat/lng, accuracy, distance, geofence result,
  and the target address label. Do not send this location evidence to ADP.

---

## 2. ADP integration reference (what the flow needs)

This is the resource pack the implementer needs. All of it goes into the Session 3 prompt.

### 2.1 Auth model (stable â€” build now)
- **OAuth 2.0 `client_credentials` grant + mutual TLS (mTLS).** ADP issues a client
  `x.509` certificate + private key in addition to `client_id` / `client_secret`. Every call
  (token + API) presents the client cert at the TLS layer.
- Token endpoint: `POST https://accounts.adp.com/auth/oauth/v2/token`
  body `grant_type=client_credentials&client_id=...&client_secret=...`, client cert on the
  socket. Returns a bearer `access_token` (~1h TTL). Cache + refresh on expiry.
- API base: `https://api.adp.com` (sandbox base URL differs â€” ADP will give it; keep it in env).
- Every API call: `Authorization: Bearer <token>` + the same client cert.

### 2.2 Worker APIs (stable â€” build now)
- **List workers (initial load):** `GET /hr/v2/workers` â€” paginated (`$top`, `$skip`).
  Returns each worker's `associateOID`, `workerID`, legal name, status. We map
  `associateOID` â†’ our `employeeProfiles.adpAssociateOid`.
- **Get one worker:** `GET /hr/v2/workers/{aoid}`.
- **Create / hire a worker (new caregiver â†’ ADP):** ADP worker creation is **event-based**:
  `POST /events/hr/v1/worker.hire` (a hire event payload). Returns/forms an `associateOID`
  once processed. Treat as async: submit event â†’ poll/receive the resulting worker â†’ store the AOID.

### 2.3 Time punches (ISOLATED â€” adapter, fill in on confirmation)
- The clock-in/clock-out punch POST is the **only** part whose exact endpoint/payload we leave
  as an adapter stub, because it's product-dependent (Section 1.2). The adapter interface is
  concrete; its `postPunch()` body is the fill-in. Likely shapes (confirm one):
  - Real-time event: `POST /events/time/v1/time-entry.create` (or `.modify`) with worker AOID +
    punch timestamp + in/out indicator.
  - Time card read-back for verification: `GET /time/v2/workers/{aoid}/time-cards`.
- We make outbound punches **idempotent** keyed on `(shiftId, punchType)` and store the
  returned ADP id, so a retry never double-punches.

### 2.4 Credentials â†’ Convex env vars (set when they arrive â€” nothing hardcoded)
`ADP_BASE_URL`, `ADP_TOKEN_URL`, `ADP_CLIENT_ID`, `ADP_CLIENT_SECRET`, `ADP_CLIENT_CERT_PEM`,
`ADP_CLIENT_KEY_PEM`. Set via `npx convex env set ...`. Until they exist, the integration runs
in **"pending_credentials"** mode (Section 3.3) â€” the app works fully, punches queue instead
of failing.

### 2.5 Official docs to read on credential day
`https://developers.adp.com` â†’ API Explorer â†’ "HR / Worker" and "Time & Attendance" product
guides (JS-gated portal; read in a normal browser when you have the dev-account login). The
worker + auth specs there will match Section 2.1â€“2.2; use them to confirm the punch payload in 2.3.

---

## 3. Architecture additions (so every session prompt is consistent)

### 3.1 Schema additions (Session 1) â€” extend, never rename prod tables
- `clients`: add optional `serviceAddress` object with human address fields plus optional
  `latitude`/`longitude`. The address is the default geofence target for that client's shifts.
- `shifts`: add optional `clockInAt`, `clockOutAt`, and optional `serviceLocationOverride`
  object (`label`, `addressLine`, `latitude`, `longitude`, `radiusMeters`) for a one-off address
  different from the client's normal address.
- New table **`tenantSettings`** or equivalent tenant-scoped settings table if one does not
  exist yet: include `shiftGeofence` config `{ enabled, enforceClockIn, enforceClockOut,
  defaultRadiusMeters, maxAccuracyMeters }`. Admins/coordinators can edit this. Default: off.
- New table **`timePunches`**: `tenantId`, `shiftId`, `caregiverId`, `punchType`
  (`'clock_in' | 'clock_out'`), `at` (ISO string), `source` (`'atriax'`), optional `location`
  object (`latitude`, `longitude`, `accuracyMeters`, `distanceMeters`, `targetLabel`,
  `targetLatitude`, `targetLongitude`, `withinGeofence`), `adpSyncStatus`
  (`'pending_credentials' | 'queued' | 'synced' | 'error'`), `adpPunchId` (optional),
  `adpError` (optional), `createdAt`. Indexes: `by_tenant_shift`, `by_tenant_sync_status`.
- New table **`employeeProfiles`**: `tenantId`, `clerkUserId` (optional until invite accepted),
  `tenantMemberId` (optional), `displayName`, `email`, `adpAssociateOid` (optional),
  `adpWorkerId` (optional), `adpSyncStatus` (`'pending_credentials' | 'queued' | 'synced' |
  'error' | 'matched'`), `adpError` (optional), `createdAt`. Indexes: `by_tenant`,
  `by_tenant_clerk_user`, `by_tenant_adp_aoid`.
- New table **`integrationConnections`**: `tenantId`, `provider` (`'adp'`), `status`
  (`'pending_credentials' | 'configured' | 'error'`), `lastCheckedAt`, `note`. Index `by_tenant_provider`.
- New table **`integrationEvents`**: `tenantId`, `provider`, `kind` (e.g. `'worker.load'`,
  `'worker.create'`, `'punch.sync'`), `refId` (shiftId/employeeProfileId), `idempotencyKey`,
  `status`, `request` / `response` (sanitized), `createdAt`. Index `by_tenant_idemp`, `by_tenant_created`.
- Run `npx convex codegen` after; never hand-edit `convex/_generated`. Every function goes
  through `convex/authHelpers.ts` (`requireTenant` / `requireTenantRole` / `assertTenantDoc`).

### 3.2 Clock-in/out + geofence gating (Session 2) â€” server-enforced, twice
- `clockIn(shiftId, location?)` â€” caregiver-only, tenant-guarded. If geofence is enabled for
  the tenant/shift, the mutation **requires** browser-provided `latitude`, `longitude`, and
  `accuracyMeters`, validates them server-side against the client service address or shift
  override using a Haversine distance helper, and rejects if outside radius or if accuracy is
  worse than `maxAccuracyMeters`. If geofence is disabled, location is optional. On success it
  records `clockInAt` + a `timePunches` clock_in row with the location evidence, transitions
  `scheduled/in_progress` as per `shiftLifecycle.ts`, writes `auditEvents`, and **enqueues**
  an ADP punch sync (Section 3.3). The note becomes editable ONLY after a clock_in exists
  (server rejects note writes otherwise).
- `clockOut(shiftId, location?)` â€” caregiver-only. If geofence is enabled, validates location
  the same way as clock-in. Also **rejects** unless the shift note passes the existing
  `convex/shiftValidation.ts` completeness check (start/end/services/clientResponse/narrative +
  required-proof tasks). On success records `clockOutAt` + clock_out punch row + audit + enqueues
  ADP sync, and moves the shift to `submitted`.
- Add tenant-guarded admin/coordinator mutations to update geofence settings and client/shift
  service-location data. Admins and coordinators can enable/disable the rule; caregivers cannot.
- Reuse the existing submit path for `submitted`; do not fork it.
- The clock-out gate and the geofence gate are product controls â€” both must be enforced in Convex,
  not just the UI. Browser geolocation is only input evidence; the server decides pass/fail.

### 3.3 ADP port + adapter (Session 3) â€” the plug-and-play core
- `convex/integrations/adp/adpPort.ts` â€” interface: `getAccessToken()`, `listWorkers(cursor)`,
  `createWorker(profile)`, `postPunch(punch)`, `getTimeCards(aoid)`.
- `convex/integrations/adp/adpClient.ts` â€” the real adapter: OAuth2 client_credentials + mTLS
  using the env vars (2.4), `node:https` Agent with cert/key, token cache. `postPunch()` body is
  the **single fill-in** documented in 2.3 (everything else concrete).
- `convex/integrations/adp/mockAdp.ts` â€” deterministic mock used in tests + dev.
- **Credential gate:** a tiny `isAdpConfigured()` checks the env vars + `integrationConnections.status`.
  - Not configured â†’ all outbound writes mark the row `adpSyncStatus='pending_credentials'` and
    **return success to the app** (queue, don't fail). The shift flow works 100% without ADP.
  - Configured â†’ a Convex **scheduler/action** drains queued rows: posts punch / creates worker,
    idempotent by `idempotencyKey`, stores `adpPunchId`/`adpAssociateOid` on success, sets
    `'error'` + `adpError` + bounded retry on failure.
- Plug-and-play day: set env vars (2.4) â†’ flip `integrationConnections.status='configured'` â†’
  run the initial worker-load action â†’ queued punches/workers drain automatically. No code change.

### 3.4 FE â€” pixel-faithful to the dark Figma
- Source of truth: `atriax/design/design-system-tokens.md` (exact hex/spacing/radius/type) +
  `atriax/design/design-screen-inventory.md` (frame names/IDs) + the live Figma frames.
- The caregiver wizard frames already include **Clock-In** (after Entry, unlocks the note) and
  **Clock-Out** (after the final step, gated by completed note/location) â€” see the
  figma-mcp-design skill note "INSERTING a screen into an ordered lane". So the design exists;
  we implement it, and add/confirm the location-permission/geofence blocked states in the same
  Figma style if they are not already explicit frames.
- Map shadcn/v0 idioms to the repo's hand-rolled primitives (`src/shared/ui/*`, `@/shared/lib/cn`);
  do NOT add Radix/shadcn deps. Mapping table in `atriax/design-implementation-plan.md Â§6`.
- **Non-negotiable FE fidelity workflow:** before any FE implementation session, the agent must
  connect to the talk-to-figma bridge (`mcp_figma_join_channel` + `mcp_figma_get_document_info`),
  export the exact target frames with `mcp_figma_export_node_as_image`, and produce a written
  screen spec containing: frame IDs/names, exported PNG paths, exact copy, layout dimensions,
  colors/tokens, spacing, states, and responsive behavior. kimi implements from that spec; review
  then opens the built app, screenshots it, and compares it against the exported Figma PNGs.
  A FE session is **not accepted** if the screenshots visibly diverge from Figma; the review must
  request a kimi fix pass until the visual match is approved.
- **kimi has no vision and cannot drive the Figma plugin directly.** That is fine: the Hermes
  planning/review stages own the Figma export + visual comparison; kimi only writes code from the
  frozen spec. If the Figma plugin is unavailable, do not start pixel-specific FE sessions â€” stop
  and reconnect or use already-exported PNGs from the prior pre-step.

---

## 4. Session order & prompts

Order respects the roadmap rule **data model â†’ server rules â†’ integration â†’ UI â†’ tests**. Each
"PIPELINE" session has a **COPY/PASTE INTO A NEW HERMES SESSION** block. You paste that whole block
as the first message in a fresh Hermes session; Hermes then runs the pipeline internally. "MANUAL"
sessions are agent/you actions (no code pipeline).

Dependency chain: S0 â†’ S1 â†’ S2 â†’ S3 â†’ S4 (backend done) ; S5 â†’ S6 â†’ S7 (FE) ; S8 (tests) ; S9 (runbook).
S5 can start in parallel with S1â€“S4 once the theme decision is made.

---

### SESSION 0 â€” Pre-flight (MANUAL, no pipeline)
Goal: green baseline so gates are meaningful.
Steps:
1. `cd solid-pancake`; use bundled Node v22 (`~/AppData/Local/hermes/node` on PATH), `npm install`.
2. `npm run lint && npm run typecheck && npm run test` â€” confirm all green BEFORE any change.
3. Start `npx convex dev` in a second terminal (keeps `_generated` synced).
4. Confirm the three decisions in Section 1 (theme = dark; ADP product question sent to Maria; geofence defaults accepted).
5. Confirm `pipeline.py` runs with `--plan-mode codex` and that codex/kimi CLIs are authed on disk. If a backend falls back, note it in reports.

---

Goal: all Phase-1 tables/fields, codegen, guard tests. No business logic yet.

```text
ATRIA-X Phase 1, backend schema only. Extend convex/schema.ts WITHOUT renaming or removing any
existing table. Add: (1) clients gets optional serviceAddress object { line1, line2 optional, city,
state, postalCode, country optional, latitude optional number, longitude optional number }. (2) shifts
gets optional clockInAt and clockOutAt string fields plus optional serviceLocationOverride object
{ label, addressLine optional, latitude, longitude, radiusMeters optional }. (3) Add tenant-scoped
settings (new tenantSettings table if needed) with shiftGeofence { enabled:boolean, enforceClockIn:
boolean, enforceClockOut:boolean, defaultRadiusMeters:number, maxAccuracyMeters:number }, default off.
(4) New table timePunches { tenantId: id'tenants', shiftId: id'shifts', caregiverId: string,
punchType: union('clock_in','clock_out'), at: string, source: literal'atriax', location: optional
object { latitude:number, longitude:number, accuracyMeters:number, distanceMeters:optional number,
targetLabel:optional string, targetLatitude:optional number, targetLongitude:optional number,
withinGeofence:optional boolean }, adpSyncStatus: union('pending_credentials','queued','synced','error'),
adpPunchId: optional string, adpError: optional string, createdAt: string } with indexes
by_tenant_shift [tenantId,shiftId] and by_tenant_sync_status [tenantId,adpSyncStatus]. (5) New table
employeeProfiles { tenantId, clerkUserId: optional string, tenantMemberId: optional id'tenantMembers',
displayName, email, adpAssociateOid: optional string, adpWorkerId: optional string, adpSyncStatus:

indexes by_tenant [tenantId], by_tenant_clerk_user [tenantId,clerkUserId], by_tenant_adp_aoid
[tenantId,adpAssociateOid]. (6) New table integrationConnections { tenantId, provider: literal'adp',
status: union('pending_credentials','configured','error'), lastCheckedAt: optional string, note:
optional string } index by_tenant_provider [tenantId,provider]. (7) New table integrationEvents
{ tenantId, provider: literal'adp', kind: string, refId: optional string, idempotencyKey: string,
status: string, request: optional any, response: optional any, createdAt } indexes by_tenant_idemp
```

```text
Task:

ATRIA-X Phase 1 backend. Implement caregiver clock-in/clock-out with server-side note gating
AND optional geofence gating in convex/, reusing existing convex/shiftLifecycle.ts,
convex/shiftValidation.ts, convex/shifts.ts, and the timePunches/geofence settings from the prior
session. All functions go through convex/authHelpers.ts and are caregiver-scoped (requireTenantRole
org:caregiver) with assertTenantDoc on every fetched shift/client. Add a pure tested helper
convex/locationValidation.ts with Haversine distance, target resolution (shift override beats client
serviceAddress), accuracy validation, and plain-language error messages. Add admin/coordinator
mutations to update tenant shiftGeofence settings and client/shift service-location data (require
org:admin or org:coordinator); caregivers cannot change these settings. Add: (1) mutation
clockIn({ shiftId, location? }): if geofence is enabled + enforceClockIn, REQUIRE location
{ latitude, longitude, accuracyMeters } from browser geolocation; reject if no target address coords
configured, outside radius, or accuracy > maxAccuracyMeters. If geofence is disabled, location is
optional. On success record shifts.clockInAt = now ISO, insert a timePunches row including location
evidence and {punchType:'clock_in', source:'atriax', adpSyncStatus:'pending_credentials', at: now},
apply lifecycle transition per shiftLifecycle.ts, write auditEvents, and enqueue ADP sync via an
internal action adpSyncPunch (stub/no-op for now but real call site). (2) Guard: progress-note writes
(existing note create/update path) must REJECT with a clear error unless a clock_in punch exists.
(3) mutation clockOut({ shiftId, location? }): if geofence is enabled + enforceClockOut, validate
location the same way; also REJECT unless the shift note passes convex/shiftValidation.ts completeness
(start/end/services/clientResponse/narrative + all requiredProof tasks complete). On pass record
clockOutAt, insert a clock_out timePunches row with location evidence, write auditEvents, enqueue
adpSyncPunch, and move the shift to 'submitted' reusing the existing submit path (do not fork it).
Tests: note write blocked before clock-in; clock-in blocked when geofence enabled and location missing;
clock-in blocked outside radius; clock-in succeeds inside radius and stores distance/location evidence;
clock-in succeeds without location when geofence disabled; clock-out blocked when note incomplete;
clock-out blocked outside radius when enabled; clock-out succeeds and emits clock_out punch + audit when
note complete and inside radius; idempotent double clock-in does not create two punches. Style: single
quotes, no semicolons, 2-space. Run npx convex codegen. Gates: lint, typecheck, test green.
```
Acceptance: note gate, clock-out gate, and geofence gate are enforced in Convex with passing tests; enqueue call sites real.

```text
Task:

ATRIA-X Phase 1 backend â€” ADP time integration, built plug-and-play (NO credentials yet; must run
safely without them). Create convex/integrations/adp/. (1) adpPort.ts: a TypeScript interface
AdpPort { getAccessToken(), listWorkers(cursor?), createWorker(profile), postPunch(punch),
getTimeCards(aoid) } with precise input/output types. (2) adpClient.ts: real adapter implementing
AdpPort for ADP Workforce Now. Auth = OAuth2 client_credentials + mutual TLS: POST to
process.env ADP_TOKEN_URL with grant_type=client_credentials, ADP_CLIENT_ID, ADP_CLIENT_SECRET,
presenting a client cert built from ADP_CLIENT_CERT_PEM + ADP_CLIENT_KEY_PEM via a node:https
Agent; cache the bearer token until expiry. listWorkers => GET {ADP_BASE_URL}/hr/v2/workers
paginated ($top,$skip) returning associateOID/workerID/name/status. createWorker => POST
{ADP_BASE_URL}/events/hr/v1/worker.hire (hire event). getTimeCards => GET
{ADP_BASE_URL}/time/v2/workers/{aoid}/time-cards. postPunch => leave the request body as a clearly
commented STUB (product-dependent: real-time time-entry event vs batch) that throws
NotConfiguredError if called while unconfigured, and is the single documented fill-in point; wire
everything else around it concretely. (3) mockAdp.ts: deterministic in-memory AdpPort for tests.
(4) config.ts: isAdpConfigured(ctx, tenantId) = all ADP_* env vars present AND
integrationConnections.status === 'configured' for that tenant. (5) Sync engine as Convex internal
actions: adpSyncPunch(timePunchId) and adpSyncWorker(employeeProfileId): if NOT configured, set the
row's adpSyncStatus='pending_credentials' and return (app keeps working); if configured, call the
adapter, and on success set 'synced' + store adpPunchId/adpAssociateOid, on failure set 'error' +
adpError with bounded retry. Make all outbound calls idempotent keyed on integrationEvents
idempotencyKey = `${kind}:${refId}` so retries never double-post; record each attempt (sanitized,
NO secrets) in integrationEvents. (6) An internal action adpInitialWorkerLoad(): pulls
listWorkers and upserts employeeProfiles, matching existing profiles by email/name and storing
adpAssociateOid, marking matched vs created. Use convex/authHelpers.ts for all tenant data;
NEVER log secret values. Tests (convex/*.test.ts) using mockAdp: unconfigured => punch marked
pending_credentials and app call still succeeds; configured => punch posts once, stores adpPunchId,
second drain is idempotent; worker load matches an existing profile by email and creates a new one
otherwise; createWorker enqueued when a caregiver is created. Style: single quotes, no semicolons,
2-space. npx convex codegen. Gates: lint, typecheck, test green.
```
Acceptance: with mock adapter, configured + unconfigured paths both pass; idempotency proven; no secrets logged.

```text
the 6-step note, verifies Clock Out is disabled until the note is complete then enabled, Clock Out ->
shift submitted; coordinator logs in, sees it in the review queue, opens detail, requests a correction
(caregiver fixes + resubmits), then approves -> shift becomes billing-ready. Assert status = color + word
at each step. (2) Playwright/logic coverage for geofence enabled: mock browser geolocation inside radius
=> Clock In/Out allowed and location evidence stored; mock outside radius => UI shows the Figma-style
blocked state and server rejects; deny geolocation permission => UI shows blocked state and no punch is
created; disable the setting as admin/coordinator => location is no longer requested. (3) ADP contract/
integration tests using the mock adapter: unconfigured tenant -> clock-in/out succeed and timePunches are
marked pending_credentials, app fully usable; then flip integrationConnections.status to 'configured' with
mock creds and run the sync engine -> queued punches post exactly once (idempotent), adpPunchId stored,
statuses go synced; run adpInitialWorkerLoad -> existing profiles matched by email, new ones created with
adpAssociateOid; createCaregiver enqueues a worker sync that drains to synced. Assert NO secret values or
raw ADP credentials appear in integrationEvents/logs, and location evidence is NOT included in ADP punch
payloads unless ADP explicitly requires it later. Update seed.ts if needed for e2e fixtures (idempotent).
Style: single quotes, no semicolons, 2-space. Gates: lint, typecheck, unit, e2e green; report the real
pass/fail of each.
```
Acceptance: e2e loop green; configured/unconfigured ADP both proven; idempotent; secret-safe.

---

### SESSION 9 â€” Plug-and-play runbook (MANUAL, no pipeline)
Goal: the exact steps for the day ADP credentials arrive (write this doc OUTSIDE the repo, e.g. `atriax/adp-go-live-runbook.md`).
Contents:
1. Confirm with ADP: product (WFN?), time module, punch API shape (real-time event vs batch), sandbox base URL â†’ finalize the `postPunch()` body in `adpClient.ts` (the single documented stub) via one more small pipeline run.
2. `npx convex env set` the six `ADP_*` vars (Section 2.4) in the target Convex deployment.
3. Flip `integrationConnections.status = 'configured'` for the tenant.
4. Run `runAdpInitialWorkerLoad` from the admin panel; verify matched/created counts.
5. Trigger a test shift; confirm punches reach the ADP sandbox (`getTimeCards`); confirm idempotency.
6. Monitor `integrationEvents` for errors; bounded retry handles transient failures.
Durable env fixes redeploy from CI, never from a local box.

---

## 5. Resources the flow needs (consolidated)

- **Phase-1 scope:** `atriax/delivery-phases.md`, `atriax/adp-shift-flow-for-maria.md`.
- **ADP API model:** Section 2 above (auth + worker concrete; punch isolated) + `developers.adp.com`
  on credential day to confirm the punch payload.
- **Design (FE = exactly Figma):** `atriax/design/design-system-tokens.md` (exact tokens),
  `atriax/design/design-screen-inventory.md` (frame names/IDs), the live Figma file via the
  talk-to-figma bridge (`figma-mcp-design` skill) for exporting reference PNGs. Caregiver
  wizard already has Clock-In/Clock-Out frames; add/verify geolocation permission + blocked
  states in the same style if the frames are not explicit yet.
- **Repo anchors per session:** schema `convex/schema.ts`; lifecycle `convex/shiftLifecycle.ts`;
  validation `convex/shiftValidation.ts`; shifts `convex/shifts.ts`/`shiftQueries.ts`; reviews
  `convex/reviews.ts`; members/invites `convex/members.ts`/`invitations.ts`; guards
  `convex/authHelpers.ts`; caregiver FE `src/features/caregiver/*`; coordinator FE
  `src/features/coordinator/*` + `src/features/dashboard/*`; primitives `src/shared/ui/*`;
  tokens `src/index.css`.
- **Pipeline:** `multi-model-dev-pipeline` skill; gates need Node â‰¥ 20.12 (bundled v22) + `npm install`.
- **House rules:** multi-tenancy via authHelpers (enforced twice), `npx convex codegen` after
  every backend change, never bill without complete+approved docs, style = single quotes / no
  semicolons / 2-space.

## 6. Honest caveats
- **kimi can't see Figma.** FE fidelity comes from the Hermes/opus/codex pre-step exporting frames via
  the Figma plugin, writing a precise spec, and the review stage comparing live app screenshots against
  those PNGs. If the plugin/exports are unavailable, do not start a pixel-fidelity FE session. Budget for it.
- **ADP punch endpoint is the one true unknown** â€” isolated to `adpClient.postPunch()`. Everything
  around it (auth, worker load, worker create, queueing, idempotency, UI, gating) is fully built
  and tested against a mock, so "plug and play" is real: on credential day it's env vars + a
  status flip + finalizing one function body.
- **Theme:** the plan assumes dark to match Figma. If you keep light, skip Session 5's dark port
  and accept the Figma won't match pixel-for-pixel.
- **Geolocation is a control, not proof of truth.** Browser geolocation can be denied or spoofed, so the
  server must validate all submitted location evidence and store an audit trail. This is sufficient for
  operational enforcement; it is not a fraud-proof GPS system.
- Each pipeline session is independent and resumable; report each `summary.md` honestly (model
  fallbacks + real gate results) before starting the next.

416: AdpPort { getAccessToken(), listWorkers(cursor?), createWorker(profile), postPunch(punch),
417: getTimeCards(aoid) } with precise input/output types. (2) adpClient.ts: real adapter implementing
418: AdpPort for ADP Workforce Now. Auth = OAuth2 client_credentials + mutual TLS: POST to
419: process.env ADP_TOKEN_URL with grant_type=client_credentials, ADP_CLIENT_ID, ADP_CLIENT_SECRET,
420: presenting a client cert built from ADP_CLIENT_CERT_PEM + ADP_CLIENT_KEY_PEM via a node:https
421: Agent; cache the bearer token until expiry. listWorkers => GET {ADP_BASE_URL}/hr/v2/workers
422: paginated ($top,$skip) returning associateOID/workerID/name/status. createWorker => POST
423: {ADP_BASE_URL}/events/hr/v1/worker.hire (hire event). getTimeCards => GET
424: {ADP_BASE_URL}/time/v2/workers/{aoid}/time-cards. postPunch => leave the request body as a clearly
425: commented STUB (product-dependent: real-time time-entry event vs batch) that throws
426: NotConfiguredError if called while unconfigured, and is the single documented fill-in point; wire
427: everything else around it concretely. (3) mockAdp.ts: deterministic in-memory AdpPort for tests.
428: (4) config.ts: isAdpConfigured(ctx, tenantId) = all ADP_* env vars present AND
429: integrationConnections.status === 'configured' for that tenant. (5) Sync engine as Convex internal
430: actions: adpSyncPunch(timePunchId) and adpSyncWorker(employeeProfileId): if NOT configured, set the
431: row's adpSyncStatus='pending_credentials' and return (app keeps working); if configured, call the
432: adapter, and on success set 'synced' + store adpPunchId/adpAssociateOid, on failure set 'error' +
433: adpError with bounded retry. Make all outbound calls idempotent keyed on integrationEvents
434: idempotencyKey = `${kind}:${refId}` so retries never double-post; record each attempt (sanitized,

436: listWorkers and upserts employeeProfiles, matching existing profiles by email/name and storing
437: adpAssociateOid, marking matched vs created. Use convex/authHelpers.ts for all tenant data;
438: NEVER log secret values. Tests (convex/*.test.ts) using mockAdp: unconfigured => punch marked
439: pending_credentials and app call still succeeds; configured => punch posts once, stores adpPunchId,
440: second drain is idempotent; worker load matches an existing profile by email and creates a new one
441: otherwise; createWorker enqueued when a caregiver is created. Style: single quotes, no semicolons,

import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test'
import {
  E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL,
  E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD,
  E2E_COORDINATOR_EMAIL,
  E2E_COORDINATOR_PASSWORD,
  E2E_ORG_ID,
  e2eCredentialsAvailable,
  resetE2EShifts,
  signInWithClerk,
  signOut,
} from './helpers/auth'
import { dryRunHarnessUrl } from './helpers/dryRun'

const GEOFENCE_CLIENT = 'Maya Torres'
const INSIDE_COORDS = { latitude: 44.9779, longitude: -93.2649 }
const OUTSIDE_COORDS = { latitude: 45.0, longitude: -93.0 }

test.describe.configure({ mode: 'serial' })


async function enableGeofence(page: import('@playwright/test').Page) {
  await signInWithClerk(page, E2E_COORDINATOR_EMAIL, E2E_COORDINATOR_PASSWORD, E2E_ORG_ID)
  await page.goto('/settings/geofence')
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/settings\/geofence/)

  await page.locator('[data-testid="geofence-enabled-checkbox"]').check()
  await page.locator('[data-testid="geofence-enforce-clock-in-checkbox"]').check()
  await page.locator('[data-testid="geofence-enforce-clock-out-checkbox"]').check()
  await page.locator('[data-testid="save-geofence-button"]').click()
  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="geofence-message"]')).toHaveText('Geofence settings saved.', { timeout: 10000 })
  await signOut(page)
}

async function openGeofenceShift(page: import('@playwright/test').Page) {
  await page.goto('/caregiver/today')
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/caregiver\/today/)

  const shiftCard = page.locator(`[data-testid^="shift-card-"]`, { hasText: GEOFENCE_CLIENT })
  await expect(shiftCard).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 })
  await shiftCard.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-start-button"]').click()
  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="shift-clock-in-screen"]')).toBeVisible()
}

if (e2eCredentialsAvailable()) {
  test.afterEach(async ({ page }) => {
    // Restore fixture shifts to a clean scheduled state and disable geofence
    // so each scenario starts from the same baseline.
    await resetE2EShifts(page)
  })

  test('geofence inside radius allows clock in/out and stores location evidence', async ({ page, context }) => {
    await enableGeofence(page)

    await context.grantPermissions(['geolocation'])
    await context.setGeolocation(INSIDE_COORDS)
    await signInWithClerk(page, E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL, E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD, E2E_ORG_ID)
    await openGeofenceShift(page)

    const locationPanel = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-location-panel"]')
    await expect(locationPanel).toBeVisible()

    await page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-button"]').click()
    await expect(locationPanel).toHaveAttribute('data-location-status', 'granted', { timeout: 15000 })
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="step-content-when"]')).toBeAttached({ timeout: 15000 })

    // Fill note quickly and clock out
    const geoWhen = page.locator('[data-testid="step-content-when"]')
    await geoWhen.locator('button:has-text("Change")').first().click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="start-time-input"]').fill('14:00')
    await geoWhen.locator('button:has-text("Change")').nth(1).click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="end-time-input"]').fill('18:00')
    await page.locator('[data-testid="wizard-next-button"]:visible').click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="service-option-Bathing"]').click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="wizard-next-button"]:visible').click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="narrative-textarea"]').fill(
      'I helped Maya with mobility exercises and a meal. She was steady on her feet.',
    )
    await page.locator('[data-testid="wizard-next-button"]:visible').click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="goal-option-Walk a little each day"]').click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="wizard-next-button"]:visible').click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="issue-choice-no"]').click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid^="task-complete-checkbox-"]').first().check()
    await page.locator('[data-testid^="task-proof-input-"]').first().setInputFiles({
      name: 'geo-proof.txt',
      mimeType: 'text/plain',
      buffer: Buffer.from('geofence proof'),
    })
    await page.locator('[data-testid="wizard-next-button"]:visible').click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="confirm-checkbox"]').check()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="wizard-next-button"]:visible').click()

    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="shift-clock-out-screen"]')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 })
    const outPanel = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-out-location-panel"]')
    await expect(outPanel).toBeVisible()
    await expect(outPanel).toHaveAttribute('data-location-status', 'granted', { timeout: 15000 })
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="clock-out-button"]')).toBeEnabled()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="clock-out-button"]').click()
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="shift-success-screen"]')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 })
  })

  test('geofence outside radius shows blocked UI and server rejects punch', async ({ page, context }) => {
    await enableGeofence(page)

    await context.grantPermissions(['geolocation'])
    await context.setGeolocation(OUTSIDE_COORDS)
    await signInWithClerk(page, E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL, E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD, E2E_ORG_ID)
    await openGeofenceShift(page)

    const locationPanel = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-location-panel"]')
    await expect(locationPanel).toBeVisible()

    const clockInButton = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-button"]')
    await clockInButton.click()
    await expect(locationPanel).toHaveAttribute('data-location-status', 'outside', { timeout: 15000 })
    await expect(clockInButton).toBeDisabled()
  })

  test('denied geolocation permission shows blocked UI and creates no punch', async ({ page, context }) => {
    await enableGeofence(page)

    await context.clearPermissions()
    await signInWithClerk(page, E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL, E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD, E2E_ORG_ID)
    await openGeofenceShift(page)

    const locationPanel = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-location-panel"]')
    await expect(locationPanel).toBeVisible()

    const clockInButton = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-button"]')
    await clockInButton.click()
    await expect(locationPanel).toHaveAttribute('data-location-status', 'denied', { timeout: 15000 })
    await expect(clockInButton).toBeDisabled()
  })

  test('disabling geofence stops requesting browser location', async ({ page, context }) => {
    // Geofence is already disabled by the afterEach reset.
    await context.clearPermissions()
    await signInWithClerk(page, E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL, E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD, E2E_ORG_ID)
    await openGeofenceShift(page)

    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-location-panel"]')).toHaveCount(0)
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-button"]')).toBeEnabled()
  })

} else {
  test('geofence inside radius allows clock in/out and stores location evidence (dry-run)', async ({ page, context }) => {
    await context.grantPermissions(['geolocation'])
    await context.setGeolocation(INSIDE_COORDS)
    await page.goto(dryRunHarnessUrl('geofence.html?view=settings'))

    await page.locator('[data-testid="geofence-enabled-checkbox"]').check()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="geofence-enforce-clock-in-checkbox"]').check()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="geofence-enforce-clock-out-checkbox"]').check()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="save-geofence-button"]').click()
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="geofence-message"]')).toHaveText('Geofence settings saved.')

    await page.goto(dryRunHarnessUrl('geofence.html?view=caregiver-list&geofence=enabled'))
    await page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-start-button"]').click()
    const locationPanel = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-location-panel"]')
    await expect(locationPanel).toBeVisible()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-button"]').click()
    await expect(locationPanel).toHaveAttribute('data-location-status', 'granted', { timeout: 15000 })
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="step-content-when"]')).toBeAttached()

    for (let index = 0; index < 6; index += 1) {
      await page.locator('[data-testid="wizard-next-button"]:visible').click()
    }
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="shift-clock-out-screen"]')).toBeVisible()
    const outPanel = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-out-location-panel"]')
    await expect(outPanel).toHaveAttribute('data-location-status', 'granted', { timeout: 15000 })
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="clock-out-button"]')).toBeEnabled()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="clock-out-button"]').click()
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="shift-success-screen"]')).toBeVisible()
  })

  test('geofence outside radius shows blocked UI and server rejects punch (dry-run)', async ({ page, context }) => {
    await context.grantPermissions(['geolocation'])
    await context.setGeolocation(OUTSIDE_COORDS)
    await page.goto(dryRunHarnessUrl('geofence.html?view=caregiver-list&geofence=enabled'))
    await page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-start-button"]').click()
    const locationPanel = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-location-panel"]')
    await expect(locationPanel).toBeVisible()
    const clockInButton = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-button"]')
    await clockInButton.click()
    await expect(locationPanel).toHaveAttribute('data-location-status', 'outside', { timeout: 15000 })
    await expect(clockInButton).toBeDisabled()
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="step-content-when"]')).toHaveClass(/hidden/)
  })

  test('denied geolocation permission shows blocked UI and creates no punch (dry-run)', async ({ page, context }) => {
    await context.clearPermissions()
    await page.goto(dryRunHarnessUrl('geofence.html?view=caregiver-list&geofence=enabled'))
    await page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-start-button"]').click()
    const locationPanel = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-location-panel"]')
    await expect(locationPanel).toBeVisible()
    const clockInButton = page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-button"]')
    await clockInButton.click()
    await expect(locationPanel).toHaveAttribute('data-location-status', 'denied', { timeout: 15000 })
    await expect(clockInButton).toBeDisabled()
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="step-content-when"]')).toHaveClass(/hidden/)
  })

  test('disabling geofence stops requesting browser location (dry-run)', async ({ page, context }) => {
    await context.clearPermissions()
    await page.goto(dryRunHarnessUrl('geofence.html?view=caregiver-list'))
    await page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-start-button"]').click()
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-location-panel"]')).toHaveCount(1)
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-location-panel"]')).toHaveClass(/hidden/)
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-button"]')).toBeEnabled()
  })
}

import { resolve } from 'node:path'
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url'

export function dryRunHarnessUrl(filename: string): string {
  const [pathPart, queryPart] = filename.split('?')
  const filePath = resolve(process.cwd(), 'tests', 'e2e', 'harness', pathPart)
  const url = pathToFileURL(filePath).href
  return queryPart ? `${url}?${queryPart}` : url
}

import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test'
import {
  E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL,
  E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD,
  E2E_COORDINATOR_EMAIL,
  E2E_COORDINATOR_PASSWORD,
  E2E_ORG_ID,
  e2eCredentialsAvailable,
  signInWithClerk,
  signOut,
} from './helpers/auth'
import { dryRunHarnessUrl } from './helpers/dryRun'

const CLIENT_NAME = 'Sam Lee'

test.describe.configure({ mode: 'serial' })

if (e2eCredentialsAvailable()) {
  test('full caregiver-to-billing lifecycle with geofence disabled', async ({ page, context }) => {
    // Ensure geofence is disabled for this flow
    await context.clearPermissions()

    // ---- Caregiver: clock in ----
    await signInWithClerk(page, E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL, E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD, E2E_ORG_ID)
    await page.goto('/caregiver/today')
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/caregiver\/today/)

    const shiftCard = page.locator('[data-testid^="shift-card-"][data-shift-status="scheduled"]', {
      hasText: CLIENT_NAME,
    })
    await expect(shiftCard).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 })

    // Assert initial status word + color
    const statusBadge = shiftCard.locator('[data-testid="shift-status-badge"]')
    await expect(statusBadge).toHaveText('UPCOMING')
    await expect(statusBadge).toHaveClass(/bg-atria-info/)

    await page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-start-button"]:visible').click()
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="shift-clock-in-screen"]')).toBeVisible()

    await page.locator('[data-testid="clock-in-button"]').click()
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="step-content-when"]')).toBeAttached({ timeout: 15000 })

    // ---- Complete 6-step note ----
    // Step 1: When (time inputs render after clicking Change)
    const whenStep = page.locator('[data-testid="step-content-when"]')
    await whenStep.locator('button:has-text("Change")').first().click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="start-time-input"]').fill('09:00')
    await whenStep.locator('button:has-text("Change")').nth(1).click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="end-time-input"]').fill('13:00')
    await page.locator('[data-testid="wizard-next-button"]:visible').click()
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="step-content-what"]')).toBeAttached()

    // Step 2: What
    await page.locator('[data-testid="service-option-Bathing"]').click()
    await page.locator('[data-testid="wizard-next-button"]:visible').click()
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="step-content-how"]')).toBeAttached()

    // Step 3: How
    await page.locator('[data-testid="narrative-textarea"]').fill(
      'I helped Sam with a warm bath, breakfast, and light housekeeping. He was in good spirits and ate a full meal.',

    await page.locator('[data-testid="goal-option-Walk a little each day"]').click()

    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="step-content-issues"]')).toBeAttached()

    // Step 5: Issues + task proof
    await page.locator('[data-testid="issue-choice-no"]').click()

    await taskCheckbox.check()

    const proofInput = page.locator('[data-testid^="task-proof-input-"]').first()
    await proofInput.setInputFiles({
      name: 'proof.txt',
      mimeType: 'text/plain',
      buffer: Buffer.from('shift proof'),
    })
    await expect(page.locator('text=proof.txt')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 })
    await page.locator('[data-testid="wizard-next-button"]:visible').click()
    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="step-content-done"]')).toBeAttached()

    // Step 6: Done / confirm
    // Clock Out (via the wizard submit button) is disabled until the note is complete.
    const nextButton = page.locator('[data-testid="wizard-next-button"]:visible')
    await expect(nextButton).toBeDisabled()

    await page.locator('[data-testid="confirm-checkbox"]').check()
    await expect(nextButton).toBeEnabled()

    await nextButton.click()

})

import { chromium, type FullConfig } from '@playwright/test'
import {
  E2E_ADMIN_EMAIL,
  E2E_ADMIN_PASSWORD,
  E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL,
  E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD,
  E2E_COORDINATOR_EMAIL,
  E2E_COORDINATOR_PASSWORD,
  E2E_ORG_ID,
  e2eCredentialsAvailable,
  signInWithClerk,
  signOut,
} from './helpers/auth'

type RoleCredentials = {
  email: string
  password: string
}

async function extractClerkUserId(page: import('@playwright/test').Page): Promise<string | null> {
  return page.evaluate(() => {
    const clerk = (window as unknown as Record<string, unknown>).Clerk as
      | { user?: { id: string } }
      | undefined
    return clerk?.user?.id ?? null
  })
}

async function seedE2EFixtures(
  authToken: string,
  userIds: {
    adminUserId: string
    coordinatorUserId: string
    caregiverUserId: string
  },
) {
  const convexUrl = process.env.VITE_CONVEX_URL
  if (!convexUrl) {
    throw new Error('VITE_CONVEX_URL is not set; cannot seed e2e fixtures.')
  }

  const response = await fetch(`${convexUrl}/api/mutation`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      Authorization: `Bearer ${authToken}`,
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      path: 'seed:seedE2E',
      args: {
        clerkOrgId: E2E_ORG_ID,
        adminUserId: userIds.adminUserId,
        coordinatorUserId: userIds.coordinatorUserId,
        caregiverUserId: userIds.caregiverUserId,
      },
      format: 'json',
    }),
  })

  if (!response.ok) {
    const text = await response.text()
    throw new Error(`seedE2E failed: ${response.status} ${text}`)
  }

  const result = await response.json()
  console.log('seedE2E result:', result)
}

async function extractClerkToken(page: import('@playwright/test').Page): Promise<string | null> {
  return page.evaluate(() => {
    const keys = ['__clerk_client_jwt', '__session', '__clerk_session_jwt']
    for (const key of keys) {
      const value = localStorage.getItem(key)
      if (value) return value
    }
    return null
  })
}

export default async function globalSetup(config: FullConfig) {
  if (!e2eCredentialsAvailable()) {
    console.log('E2E credentials not configured; skipping global seed.')
    return
  }

  const browser = await chromium.launch()
  const page = await browser.newPage({
    baseURL: config.projects[0]?.use.baseURL,
  })

  try {
    const roles: Record<string, RoleCredentials> = {
      admin: { email: E2E_ADMIN_EMAIL, password: E2E_ADMIN_PASSWORD },
      coordinator: { email: E2E_COORDINATOR_EMAIL, password: E2E_COORDINATOR_PASSWORD },
      caregiver: { email: E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL, password: E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD },
    }

    const userIds: Record<string, string> = {}
    for (const [role, credentials] of Object.entries(roles)) {
      await signInWithClerk(page, credentials.email, credentials.password, E2E_ORG_ID)
      const userId = await extractClerkUserId(page)
      if (!userId) {
        throw new Error(`Could not extract Clerk user id for ${role}.`)
      }
      userIds[role] = userId
      await signOut(page)
    }

    // Sign back in as admin so we can call the seed mutation with an admin token.
    await signInWithClerk(page, E2E_ADMIN_EMAIL, E2E_ADMIN_PASSWORD, E2E_ORG_ID)

    // Bootstrap the tenant if this is the first run against this org.
    await page.goto('/select-agency')
    await page.waitForLoadState('networkidle')

    const token = await extractClerkToken(page)
    if (!token) {
      throw new Error('Could not extract Clerk session token; cannot seed e2e fixtures.')
    }

    await seedE2EFixtures(token, {
      adminUserId: userIds.admin,
      coordinatorUserId: userIds.coordinator,
      caregiverUserId: userIds.caregiver,
    })
  } finally {
    await browser.close()
  }
}

 succeeded in 737ms:
import { expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test'

export const E2E_ORG_ID = process.env.E2E_CLERK_ORG_ID ?? ''
export const E2E_ADMIN_EMAIL = process.env.E2E_ADMIN_EMAIL ?? ''
export const E2E_ADMIN_PASSWORD = process.env.E2E_ADMIN_PASSWORD ?? ''
export const E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL = process.env.E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL ?? ''
export const E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD = process.env.E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD ?? ''
export const E2E_COORDINATOR_EMAIL = process.env.E2E_COORDINATOR_EMAIL ?? ''
export const E2E_COORDINATOR_PASSWORD = process.env.E2E_COORDINATOR_PASSWORD ?? ''

export function e2eCredentialsAvailable(): boolean {
  return Boolean(
    E2E_ORG_ID &&
      E2E_ADMIN_EMAIL &&
      E2E_ADMIN_PASSWORD &&
      E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL &&
      E2E_CAREGIVER_PASSWORD &&
      E2E_COORDINATOR_EMAIL &&
      E2E_COORDINATOR_PASSWORD,
  )
}

export function assertE2ECredentialsConfigured(): void {
  if (!e2eCredentialsAvailable()) {
    throw new Error(
      'E2E Clerk credentials are not configured. Set E2E_CLERK_ORG_ID, E2E_ADMIN_EMAIL/PASSWORD, E2E_CAREGIVER_EMAIL/PASSWORD, and E2E_COORDINATOR_EMAIL/PASSWORD.',
    )
  }
}

async function selectOrgIfAsked(page: Page, orgId: string) {
  // Clerk's organization switcher may appear after sign-in. Try to select the
  // expected org by its id or name, or just wait for the app to settle.
  const orgButton = page.locator(`[data-testid="organization-button-${orgId}"]`).or(
    page.locator(`button:has-text("${orgId}")`),
  )
  if (await orgButton.isVisible().catch(() => false)) {
    await orgButton.click()
    await page.waitForLoadState('networkidle')
  }
}

export async function signInWithClerk(
  page: Page,
  email: string,
  password: string,
  orgId: string,
) {
  await page.goto('/sign-in')
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/sign-in/)

  // Clerk sign-in flow: email -> continue -> password -> continue
  const emailInput = page.locator('input[name="identifier"], input[type="email"], input[inputmode="email"]').first()
  await expect(emailInput).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 })
  await emailInput.fill(email)

  const continueButton = page.locator('button:has-text("Continue"), button[type="submit"]').first()
  await continueButton.click()

  const passwordInput = page.locator('input[name="password"], input[type="password"]').first()
  await expect(passwordInput).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 })
  await passwordInput.fill(password)

  const submitButton = page.locator('button:has-text("Continue"), button[type="submit"]').first()
  await submitButton.click()

  // Wait for navigation away from sign-in
  await expect(page).not.toHaveURL(/sign-in/, { timeout: 20000 })
  await page.waitForLoadState('networkidle')

  await selectOrgIfAsked(page, orgId)
}

export async function signOut(page: Page) {
  // Clear Clerk session state so the next sign-in starts fresh.
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    localStorage.clear()
    sessionStorage.clear()
  })
  await page.context().clearCookies()
  await page.goto('/sign-in')
  await page.waitForLoadState('networkidle')
}

async function extractClerkToken(page: Page): Promise<string | null> {
  return page.evaluate(() => {
    const keys = ['__clerk_client_jwt', '__session', '__clerk_session_jwt']
    for (const key of keys) {
      const value = localStorage.getItem(key)
      if (value) return value
    }
    return null
  })
}

export async function resetE2EShifts(page: Page) {
  assertE2ECredentialsConfigured()

  // Ensure a clean session before signing in as admin; the caller may still be
  // signed in as a caregiver or coordinator.
  await signOut(page)
  await signInWithClerk(page, E2E_ADMIN_EMAIL, E2E_ADMIN_PASSWORD, E2E_ORG_ID)

  const convexUrl = process.env.VITE_CONVEX_URL
  if (!convexUrl) {
    throw new Error('VITE_CONVEX_URL is not set; cannot reset e2e fixtures.')
  }

  const token = await extractClerkToken(page)
  if (!token) {
    throw new Error('Could not extract Clerk session token; cannot reset e2e fixtures.')
  }

  const response = await fetch(`${convexUrl}/api/mutation`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      path: 'seed:resetE2EShifts',
      args: { clerkOrgId: E2E_ORG_ID },
      format: 'json',
    }),
  })

  if (!response.ok) {
    const text = await response.text()
    throw new Error(`resetE2EShifts failed: ${response.status} ${text}`)
  }

  const result = await response.json()
  console.log('resetE2EShifts result:', result)

  await signOut(page)
}

# ATRIA-X Phase 1 Session 8 â€” Implementation Plan

## Goal

Implement and verify end-to-end and integration test coverage for the complete caregiver-to-billing shift lifecycle, geofence enforcement, and ADP mock integration contracts.

## Acceptance Criteria

- `npm run e2e` covers the full seeded lifecycle with geofence disabled:
  - Caregiver clocks in.
  - Completes the 6-step note.
  - Clock Out disabled until note complete, then enabled.
  - Clock Out submits the shift.
  - Coordinator requests a correction.
  - Caregiver fixes and resubmits.
  - Coordinator approves â†’ shift becomes billing-ready.
  - Each lifecycle state asserts both status word and status color.

- Geofence coverage verifies:
  - Inside radius allows clock in/out and stores location evidence.
  - Outside radius shows blocked UI and server rejects the punch.
  - Denied geolocation shows blocked UI and creates no punch.
  - Disabling geofence prevents location requests.

- ADP contract/integration tests verify:
  - Unconfigured tenant keeps app usable and marks punches `pending_credentials`.
  - Configured mock ADP sync posts queued punches exactly once.
  - `adpPunchId` stored and statuses become `synced`.
  - Initial worker load matches existing profiles by email and creates missing profiles with `adpAssociateOid`.
  - `createCaregiver` enqueues worker sync and drains to `synced`.
  - No secrets/raw credentials appear in `integrationEvents` or logs.
  - Location evidence is not sent in ADP punch payloads.

- Required gates genuinely green:
  - `npx convex codegen` if Convex API/schema changed
  - `npm run lint`
  - `npm run typecheck`
  - `npm run test`
  - `npm run e2e`

## Stages

### Stage 1 â€” Fixtures & selectors

- [x] Inspect existing auth, route guards, seed helpers, and test setup.
- [x] Update `convex/seed.ts` with deterministic e2e fixtures:
  - `seedE2E` mutation that creates/updates a test tenant, members (admin, coordinator, caregiver), clients with service addresses, and shifts for lifecycle + geofence scenarios.
  - Accepts the real Clerk user IDs for the configured test accounts instead of hard-coding placeholder IDs.
  - Resets fixture shifts, reviews, billing lines, time punches, and tasks to a clean scheduled baseline on every run.
  - Resets geofence settings to disabled on every run.
  - `resetE2EShifts` mutation for serial e2e scenarios to restore the baseline between tests.
- [x] Add `data-testid` attributes to key UI surfaces:
  - `CaregiverTodayPage` shift cards and "Clock in & start" button.
  - `ShiftClockInScreen`, `ShiftClockOutScreen`, `LocationStatusPanel`.
  - `ShiftNoteStep` step navigation and inputs.
  - `CoordinatorReviewPage` queue rows and filter tabs.
  - `ReviewDetail` approve / request-correction buttons and comment field.
  - `GeofenceSettingsPage` toggles and save button.

### Stage 2 â€” Server-side tests

- [x] `convex/shiftClock.test.ts`: add convex-test cases for geofence disabled, inside radius, outside radius, missing/denied location, disabled setting, and verify no punch is created on rejection.
- [x] `convex/reviews.test.ts`: add cases for correction request, resubmission returning to queue, and approval marking billing-ready only when documentation is complete.
- [x] `convex/adpSync.test.ts`: verify unconfigured tenant behavior, configured mock sync idempotency, secret redaction, and location evidence omission from ADP payloads.
- [x] `convex/employeeProfiles.test.ts`: verify `adpInitialWorkerLoad` matching/creation and `createCaregiver` enqueue + drain.

### Stage 3 â€” E2E specs

- [x] `tests/e2e/phase1-lifecycle.spec.ts`: full lifecycle using seeded data with geofence disabled.
  - Conditional auth via Clerk sign-in using `E2E_*` environment variables.
  - Required specs now fail with a clear error when credentials are unavailable instead of being skipped, so the gate is honest.
  - Added assertions: Clock Out / submit button disabled before confirmation, status word + color for correction/resubmission/approval, and `data-shift-status="billing_ready"` after approval.
- [x] `tests/e2e/geofence.spec.ts`: inside radius, outside radius, denied permission, and disable-setting flows.
  - Outside/denied scenarios now click Clock In so location is actually requested and the blocked state is exercised.
  - Each geofence test that needs enforcement enables it explicitly.
  - `afterEach` calls `seed:resetE2EShifts` so serial tests always start from the scheduled fixture baseline.
- [x] `tests/e2e/helpers/auth.ts`: shared Clerk sign-in helper, credential guard, and `resetE2EShifts` test helper.

### Stage 4 â€” Reviewer fixes (Iteration 2)

- [x] Fix lifecycle e2e interactions:
  - Click `Change` before filling start/end time inputs in `phase1-lifecycle.spec.ts` and `geofence.spec.ts`.
  - Advance through each wizard step in the correction flow instead of jumping to `done`.
- [x] Make geofence cleanup session-safe by signing out in `resetE2EShifts` before admin sign-in.
- [x] Prevent ADP side effects when caregiver invitation fails:
  - Validate `CLERK_SECRET_KEY` before creating the employee profile.
  - Delete the profile if the Clerk invitation fails.
  - Add unit tests for missing secret key and failed invitation cleanup.

### Stage 5 â€” Gates & summary

- [x] Run `npx convex codegen` if Convex files changed.
- [x] Run `npm run lint`.
- [x] Run `npm run typecheck`.
- [x] Run `npm run test`.
- [x] Run `npm run e2e`.
- [x] Write real pass/fail results to `.hermes-pipeline/20260626_161701/summary.md`.

### Stage 6 â€” Reviewer fixes (Iteration 3)

- [x] Make E2E fixtures deterministic across time:
  - Replace the current-UTC date in `seedE2E`/`resetE2EShifts` with the fixed `REF_TODAY` constant.
  - Ensure seeded lifecycle and geofence shifts are always in the past so `clockIn` cannot be rejected for "future shift".
- [x] Run `npx convex codegen` against the local `anonymous-agent` backend and report the real PASS result.
- [x] Document the required `E2E_*` credential variables in `.env.example`.
- [ ] E2E lifecycle/geofence specs still require real Clerk test-account credentials to turn green.

## Files To Create / Modify

Create:

- `.hermes-pipeline/20260626_161701/implementation-plan.md`
- `.hermes-pipeline/20260626_161701/summary.md`
- `tests/e2e/phase1-lifecycle.spec.ts`
- `tests/e2e/geofence.spec.ts`
- `tests/e2e/helpers/auth.ts`

Modify:

- `convex/seed.ts`
- `src/features/coordinator/pages/CoordinatorReviewPage.tsx`
- `src/features/dashboard/pages/DashboardPage.tsx`
- `tests/e2e/global-setup.ts`
- `convex/_generated/*` (via `npx convex codegen`, never by hand)

## Risks & Mitigations

- **Auth dependency for e2e**: Real Clerk sign-in requires test accounts. Mitigation: global setup seeds fixtures only when credentials are present; required specs fail fast with a clear message when credentials are missing so the gate cannot be green by skipping.
- **Multi-tenancy**: All backend helpers use `authHelpers` guards; no changes that weaken tenant isolation.
- **HIPAA/privacy**: Location evidence stays internal; ADP payload assertions ensure no leakage.
- **Secret leakage**: ADP mock creds are stubbed via `vi.stubEnv` and redacted by `sanitizeError`; assertions verify they do not appear in events/logs.
- **Billing invariant**: Approval only reaches `billing_ready` when documentation is complete; enforced by `reviews.approve` and verified by tests.

import { v } from 'convex/values'
import { query, mutation } from './_generated/server'
import { assertTenantDoc, requireTenant, requireTenantRole } from './authHelpers'

export const DEFAULT_SHIFT_GEOFENCE = {
  enabled: false,
  enforceClockIn: false,
  enforceClockOut: false,
  defaultRadiusMeters: 100,
  maxAccuracyMeters: 50,
}

export const get = query({
  args: { clerkOrgId: v.string() },
  handler: async (ctx, { clerkOrgId }) => {
    const { tenantId } = await requireTenant(ctx, clerkOrgId)

    const settings = await ctx.db
      .query('tenantSettings')
      .withIndex('by_tenant', (q) => q.eq('tenantId', tenantId))
      .unique()

    if (!settings) {
      return {
        tenantId,
        shiftGeofence: DEFAULT_SHIFT_GEOFENCE,
      }
    }

    assertTenantDoc(settings, tenantId)
    return settings
  },
})

export const updateShiftGeofence = mutation({
  args: {
    clerkOrgId: v.string(),
    enabled: v.boolean(),
    enforceClockIn: v.boolean(),
    enforceClockOut: v.boolean(),
    defaultRadiusMeters: v.number(),
    maxAccuracyMeters: v.number(),
  },
  handler: async (ctx, args) => {
    const { tenantId } = await requireTenantRole(ctx, args.clerkOrgId, [
      'org:admin',
      'org:coordinator',
    ])

    if (!Number.isFinite(args.defaultRadiusMeters) || args.defaultRadiusMeters <= 0) {
      throw new Error('defaultRadiusMeters must be greater than zero.')
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(args.maxAccuracyMeters) || args.maxAccuracyMeters <= 0) {
      throw new Error('maxAccuracyMeters must be greater than zero.')
    }

    const existing = await ctx.db
      .query('tenantSettings')
      .withIndex('by_tenant', (q) => q.eq('tenantId', tenantId))
      .unique()

    const shiftGeofence = {
      enabled: args.enabled,
      enforceClockIn: args.enforceClockIn,
      enforceClockOut: args.enforceClockOut,
      defaultRadiusMeters: args.defaultRadiusMeters,
      maxAccuracyMeters: args.maxAccuracyMeters,
    }

    if (existing) {
      assertTenantDoc(existing, tenantId)
      await ctx.db.patch(existing._id, { shiftGeofence })
      return existing._id
    }

    return await ctx.db.insert('tenantSettings', {
      tenantId,
      shiftGeofence,
    })
  },
})

import { v } from 'convex/values'
import { query } from './_generated/server'
import { assertTenantDoc, requireTenantRole } from './authHelpers'
import type { TenantRole } from './authHelpers'

type PunchActor = {
  role: TenantRole
  clerkUserId: string
}

export function assertPunchAuthorized(
  actor: PunchActor,
  caregiverId: string,
) {
  if (actor.role === 'org:caregiver' && actor.clerkUserId !== caregiverId) {
    throw new Error('Caregivers can only act on their assigned shifts.')
  }
}

export const get = query({
  args: { clerkOrgId: v.string(), punchId: v.id('timePunches') },
  handler: async (ctx, { clerkOrgId, punchId }) => {
    const { tenantId, identity, role } = await requireTenantRole(
      ctx,
      clerkOrgId,
      ['org:admin', 'org:coordinator', 'org:caregiver'],
    )

    const punch = await ctx.db.get(punchId)
    if (!punch) throw new Error('Time punch not found.')
    assertTenantDoc(punch, tenantId)
    assertPunchAuthorized(
      { role, clerkUserId: identity.subject },
      punch.caregiverId,
    )
    return punch
  },
})

import { v } from 'convex/values'
import { mutation } from './_generated/server'
import { requireTenantRole, assertTenantDoc } from './authHelpers'
import { internal } from './_generated/api'
import {
  calculateDocumentedHours,
  roundCurrency,
  validateShiftDocumentation,
} from './shiftValidation'

export const approve = mutation({
  args: {
    clerkOrgId: v.string(),
    shiftId: v.id('shifts'),
    comment: v.string(),
  },
  handler: async (ctx, args) => {
    const { tenantId, identity } = await requireTenantRole(
      ctx,
      args.clerkOrgId,
      ['org:coordinator', 'org:admin'],
    )

    const shift = await ctx.db.get(args.shiftId)
    if (!shift) throw new Error('Shift not found.')
    assertTenantDoc(shift, tenantId)

    if (shift.status !== 'submitted') {
      throw new Error('Only submitted shifts can be approved.')
    }

    const note = await ctx.db
      .query('progressNotes')
      .withIndex('by_tenant_shift', (q) =>
        q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('shiftId', args.shiftId),
      )
      .unique()

    if (!note) throw new Error('Progress note not found.')
    assertTenantDoc(note, tenantId)

    const tasks = await ctx.db
      .query('shiftTasks')
      .withIndex('by_tenant_shift', (q) =>
        q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('shiftId', args.shiftId),
      )
      .collect()

    for (const task of tasks) assertTenantDoc(task, tenantId)

    const blockers = validateShiftDocumentation(note, tasks)
    if (blockers.length > 0) {
      throw new Error(
        `Incomplete documentation cannot be approved: ${blockers.join(' ')}`,
      )
    }

    const hours = calculateDocumentedHours(note.startTime, note.endTime)
    if (hours === null) {
      throw new Error('Valid documented hours are required before approval.')
    }

    await ctx.db.patch(args.shiftId, { status: 'billing_ready' })

    await ctx.db.insert('reviewEvents', {
      tenantId,
      shiftId: args.shiftId,
      reviewerId: identity.subject,
      decision: 'approved',
      comment: args.comment,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    })

    const amount = roundCurrency(hours * shift.rate)

    await ctx.db.insert('billingLines', {
      tenantId,
      shiftId: args.shiftId,
      hours,
      rate: shift.rate,
      amount,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    })

    await ctx.runMutation(internal.audit.record, {
      clerkOrgId: args.clerkOrgId,
      action: 'shift_approved',
      shiftId: args.shiftId,
      previousStatus: shift.status,
      nextStatus: 'billing_ready',
    })

    return args.shiftId
  },
})

export const requestCorrection = mutation({
  args: {
    clerkOrgId: v.string(),
    shiftId: v.id('shifts'),
    comment: v.string(),
  },
  handler: async (ctx, args) => {
    const { tenantId, identity } = await requireTenantRole(
      ctx,
      args.clerkOrgId,
      ['org:coordinator', 'org:admin'],
    )

    const shift = await ctx.db.get(args.shiftId)
    if (!shift) throw new Error('Shift not found.')
    assertTenantDoc(shift, tenantId)

    if (!args.comment || args.comment.trim().length === 0) {
      throw new Error('A comment is required to request a correction.')
    }

    if (shift.status !== 'submitted' && shift.status !== 'billing_ready') {
      throw new Error(
        'Can only return submitted or approved shifts for correction.',
      )
    }

    if (shift.status === 'billing_ready') {
      const billingLines = await ctx.db
        .query('billingLines')
        .withIndex('by_tenant_shift', (q) =>
          q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('shiftId', args.shiftId),
        )
        .collect()

      for (const line of billingLines) {
        assertTenantDoc(line, tenantId)
        if (line.exportBatchId) {
          throw new Error(
            'Invoiced billing lines cannot be returned for correction without a reversal.',
          )
        }
        await ctx.db.delete(line._id)
      }
    }

    await ctx.db.patch(args.shiftId, { status: 'needs_correction' })

    await ctx.db.insert('reviewEvents', {
      tenantId,
      shiftId: args.shiftId,
      reviewerId: identity.subject,
      decision: 'correction_requested',
      comment: args.comment,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    })

    await ctx.runMutation(internal.audit.record, {
      clerkOrgId: args.clerkOrgId,
      action: 'shift_correction_requested',
      shiftId: args.shiftId,
      previousStatus: shift.status,
      nextStatus: 'needs_correction',
    })

    return args.shiftId
  },
})

import { ConvexError, v } from 'convex/values'
import { query } from './_generated/server'
import {
  assertTenantDoc,
  requireTenant,
  requireTenantRole,
} from './authHelpers'

function assertShiftReadableByRole(
  shift: { caregiverId: string },
  role: string,
  clerkUserId: string,
) {
  if (role === 'org:caregiver' && shift.caregiverId !== clerkUserId) {
    throw new ConvexError(
      'Forbidden: caregivers can only view assigned shifts.',
    )
  }
}

export const listMyShifts = query({
  args: { clerkOrgId: v.string() },
  handler: async (ctx, { clerkOrgId }) => {
    const { tenantId, identity } = await requireTenant(ctx, clerkOrgId)

    const shifts = await ctx.db
      .query('shifts')
      .withIndex('by_tenant_caregiver_status', (q) =>
        q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('caregiverId', identity.subject),
      )
      .order('desc')
      .take(100)

    const clients = await Promise.all(
      shifts.map((shift) => ctx.db.get(shift.clientId)),
    )

    clients.forEach((client) => {
      if (client) assertTenantDoc(client, tenantId)
    })

    return shifts.map((shift, index) => ({
      shift,
      client: clients[index],
    }))
  },
})

export const listForReview = query({
  args: { clerkOrgId: v.string() },
  handler: async (ctx, { clerkOrgId }) => {
    const { tenantId } = await requireTenantRole(ctx, clerkOrgId, [
      'org:admin',
      'org:coordinator',
    ])

    const submitted = await ctx.db
      .query('shifts')
      .withIndex('by_tenant_status_start', (q) =>
        q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('status', 'submitted'),
      )
      .order('desc')
      .take(100)

    const needsCorrection = await ctx.db
      .query('shifts')
      .withIndex('by_tenant_status_start', (q) =>
        q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('status', 'needs_correction'),
      )
      .order('desc')
      .take(100)

    return [...submitted, ...needsCorrection]
  },
})

export const listBillingReady = query({
  args: { clerkOrgId: v.string() },
  handler: async (ctx, { clerkOrgId }) => {
    const { tenantId } = await requireTenantRole(ctx, clerkOrgId, [
      'org:admin',
      'org:coordinator',
    ])

    return ctx.db
      .query('shifts')
      .withIndex('by_tenant_status_start', (q) =>
        q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('status', 'billing_ready'),
      )
      .order('desc')
      .take(200)
  },
})

export const listAll = query({
  args: { clerkOrgId: v.string() },
  handler: async (ctx, { clerkOrgId }) => {
    const { tenantId } = await requireTenantRole(ctx, clerkOrgId, [
      'org:admin',
      'org:coordinator',
    ])

    return ctx.db
      .query('shifts')
      .withIndex('by_tenant_status_start', (q) => q.eq('tenantId', tenantId))
      .order('desc')
      .take(200)
  },
